\par\vspace{4mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0669-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-body-L022}{}{\Indent Quae ad fontem, ut ita dicam, \textit{Ptolemaicum} in longitudinibus pertinent sunt Salé, Ar-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-body-L023}{}{zila, Mérida, Medinaceli, Zaragoza, Murcia, Calatrava.}
\par\vspace{6mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0669-H24}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-body-L024}{}{\CenterLine{\textit{Ad pag. 55-56.} — \textbf{Tabulae sinuum.}}}
\par\vspace{3mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0669-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-body-L025}{25}{\Indent Memorantur t. I, p. 10–11 (cap. III) et passim.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0669-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-body-L026}{}{\Indent Codicis errores (74 in 180 numeris minutorum et secundarum!) facile emendavi, sinus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-body-L027}{}{\Name{Albatenii} cum chordis \textit{Almagesti} (I, 9, ed. Halma; t. I, p. 38–45) iam a \Name{Delambre} cor-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-body-L028}{}{rectis (cfr. ed. Halma, notes, p. 2) comparando. Ut certior emendatio fieret adhibui etiam si-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-body-L029}{}{nuum tabulam pro singulis 15′ arcus ab \Name{Aboul Hhassan} p. 103–114 supputata; ubicum-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-body-L030}{30}{que dubitatio aliqua oriebatur tabulis nostris logarithmicis calculos iterum suscepi. — Omnia}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0669-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) Urbs et regio Sūdān occidentalis, apud Ara-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-notes_left-L002}{}{bes olim celeberrima. Urbs aut hodierna \textit{Walātah}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-notes_left-L003}{}{\ArabicRun{ولاتة} aut illi proxima; regnum, ab Atlantico ad Ni-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-notes_left-L004}{}{ger porrectum, et circa 300 Chr. conditum, anno 1203-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-notes_left-L005}{}{1204 a gente Mandingo subactum est. Vide P. C. \Name{Me-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-notes_left-L006}{}{\Name{yer}, \textit{Erforschungsgeschichte und Staatenbildun-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-notes_left-L007}{}{\textit{gen des Westsudan,} Gotha 1897, p. 59-61 (Ergän-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-notes_left-L008}{}{zungsheft nr. 121 zu Petermann’s Mitteilungen). —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-notes_left-L009}{}{Aliqua nominis similitudine decepti, putaverunt non-}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-notes_right-L001}{}{nulli Ghānah eandem esse urbem ac \textit{Ǵennē} \ArabicRun{جنى},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-notes_right-L002}{}{eo loco quo Baulē in dexteram Niger ripam influit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0669-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-notes_right-L003}{}{\Indent (2) Apud \Name{Steinschneider}, \textit{Intorno ad}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-notes_right-L004}{}{\textit{alcuni matematici del medio evo ed alle opere da}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-notes_right-L005}{}{\textit{essi composte, lettere a D. B. Boncompagni,} Roma}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-notes_right-L006}{}{1863-67, Lettera II: intorno al libro \textit{Saraceni cu-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-notes_right-L007}{}{\textit{iusdam de eris} ed al libro \textit{Tabule Jahen,} p. 10,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-notes_right-L008}{}{adn. 3. — De incerto libri auctore v. \Name{Suter}, \textit{Nach-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0669-notes_right-L009}{}{\textit{träge und Berichtigungen} p. 170.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0670}{221}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L001}{}{optime cum \Name{Ptolemaeo} congruunt; interdum discrepantiae + 1″ ab \Name{Aboul Hhassan},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L002}{}{apud quem, in sinibus integrorum et dimidiorum graduum (quos tantum examinavi), haec}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L003}{}{sunt emendanda:}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L004}{}{\hspace*{44.0mm}\makebox[7.0mm][l]{\smash{sin}}\makebox[8.0mm][r]{24\rlap{°}}\makebox[7.0mm][r]{0\rlap{′}}\makebox[6.0mm][c]{\smash{=}}\makebox[8.0mm][r]{24\rlap{\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}}}\makebox[7.0mm][r]{24\rlap{′}}\makebox[7.0mm][r]{55\rlap{″}}\makebox[34.0mm][c]{\smash{pro editionis}}\makebox[8.0mm][r]{24\rlap{\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}}}\makebox[7.0mm][r]{24\rlap{′}}\makebox[7.0mm][r]{\textit{15}\rlap{\textit{″}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L005}{5}{\hspace*{44.0mm}\makebox[7.0mm][l]{\smash{sin}}\makebox[8.0mm][r]{60\rlap{°}}\makebox[7.0mm][r]{30\rlap{′}}\makebox[6.0mm][c]{\smash{=}}\makebox[8.0mm][r]{52\rlap{\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}}}\makebox[7.0mm][r]{12\rlap{′}}\makebox[7.0mm][r]{46\rlap{″}}\makebox[34.0mm][c]{\smash{»\hspace{15mm}»}}\makebox[8.0mm][r]{52\rlap{\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}}}\makebox[7.0mm][r]{\textit{13}\rlap{\textit{′}}}\makebox[7.0mm][r]{\textit{17}\rlap{\textit{″}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L006}{}{\hspace*{44.0mm}\makebox[7.0mm][l]{\smash{sin}}\makebox[8.0mm][r]{71\rlap{°}}\makebox[7.0mm][r]{0\rlap{′}}\makebox[6.0mm][c]{\smash{=}}\makebox[8.0mm][r]{56\rlap{\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}}}\makebox[7.0mm][r]{33\rlap{′}}\makebox[7.0mm][r]{52\rlap{″}}\makebox[34.0mm][c]{\smash{»\hspace{15mm}»}}\makebox[8.0mm][r]{56\rlap{\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}}}\makebox[7.0mm][r]{\textit{43}\rlap{\textit{′}}}\makebox[7.0mm][r]{52\rlap{″.}}}
\par\vspace{6mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0670-H07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L007}{}{\CenterLine{\textit{Ad pag. 57-58.} — \textbf{Tabulae declinationis Solis.}}}
\par\vspace{3mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0670-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L008}{}{\Indent Memoratae t. I, p. 13, cap. IV.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0670-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L009}{}{\Indent Tabulae \Name{Ptolemaei} I, 12 (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 59) et \Name{Theonis} pro obliquitate 23° 51′ 20″}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L010}{10}{supputatae; tabulas igitur \Name{Albatenii} nonnisi per calculos nostris tabulis logarithmicis insti-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L011}{}{tutos \Name{Schiaparelli} emendare potuit, discrepantiis 1″ aut 2″ neglectis. Logarithmis sufficit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L012}{}{pro longitudine 1° declinationem supputare (sin \textgreek{δ} = sin L sin \textgreek{ε}); reliqui termini deducuntur dif-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L013}{}{ferentias logarithmicas sinuum pro singulis gradibus perpetuo addendo primo logarithmo; cfr.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L014}{}{\textit{notes de} M. \Name{Delambre}, apud Halma, t. I, notes p. 5–6.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0670-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L015}{15}{\Indent \textbf{\textsf{Pag. 58}}, \textit{ordines declinationis.} Tabella memoratur in fine adp. G (t. I, p. 150); de iis or-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L016}{}{dinibus cap. IV, t. I, p. 13, et etiam adnotationes meas t. I, p. 13, adn. 2, et LXXI. In codicis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L017}{}{figura perperam voces \textit{ascensus} et \textit{descensus} inter se commutatae, qua re prope ambo latera}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L018}{}{circuli amanuensis \ArabicRun{منكوس} « invertendum » scripsit; versio Hispanica (f. 43,v.) eosdem praebet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L019}{}{errores, et vocem \textit{mankūs} vertit « conuerso ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0670-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L020}{20}{\Indent \textbf{\textsf{Pag. 58}}, \textit{ascensiones rectae decadum graduum et sinus correspondentes.} Memorantur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L021}{}{adp. G, t. I, p. 150. Ascensiones ab initio Arietis numeratae, non ab initio Capricorni ut in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L022}{}{tabulis t. II, pag. 61–64 (cfr. adnot. t. I, p. 163–64 et Addenda). In titulo tertiae columnae per-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L023}{}{peram cod. \ArabicRun{ازمان ..... المنصّفة} « tempora dimidiata » loco \ArabicRun{اوتار ..... المنصّفة} « chordae dimidiatae » seu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L024}{}{sinus. — Tabulam ascensionum rectarum ad decades graduum, sed sinibus omissis et pro alia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L025}{25}{obliquitate, \Name{Ptolemaeus} quoque II, 8 (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 103) exhibet.}
\par\vspace{6mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0670-H26}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L026}{}{\CenterLine{\textit{Ad pag. 59.} — \textbf{Dierum magnitudines pro diversis latitudinibus.}}}
\par\vspace{3mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0670-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L027}{}{\Indent Cfr. cap. VIII et IX, t. I, p. 21–22.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0670-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L028}{}{\Indent Tabula a \Name{Schiaparelli} emendata. Incrementa dierum indicantur gradibus circuli aequi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L029}{}{noctialis, quorum 15° horam aequinoctialem conficiunt.}
\par\vspace{6mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0670-H30}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L030}{30}{\CenterLine{\textit{Ad pag. 60.} — \textbf{Tabula umbrarum.}}}
\par\vspace{3mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0670-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L031}{}{\Indent Memoratur cap. X, t. I, p. 22–23}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0670-P08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L032}{}{\Indent Sunt cotangentes pro radio 12 partium supputatae; tabulis logarithmicis facile potui co-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L033}{}{dicis mendas sanare. Duae primae umbrae in cod. 687\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 26′ et 663\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} (\ArabicRun{حصج} pro \ArabicRun{سمج} 343\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}) 39′;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L034}{}{sed fortasse parvae hae minutorum discrepantiae ab ipso \Name{al-Battānī} proveniunt, qui minuta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L035}{35}{illa deduxerit divisione sinuum usque ad secundas tantum radii 60 partium supputatorum.}
\par\vspace{6mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0670-H36}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L036}{}{\CenterLine{\textit{Ad pag. 61–64.} — \textbf{Ascensiones rectae et aequationes dierum.}}}
\par\vspace{3mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0670-P09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L037}{}{\Indent Tabulae memorantur cap. XIII, XXIX, XXXIV, XLII, et adp. E (ubi tabularum usus do-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0670-body-L038}{}{cetur), scilicet t. I, p. 28, 49, 72–73, 95, 148.}
\DSource{0671}{222}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0671-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L001}{}{\Indent Ascensiones rectae ab \textit{initio Capricorni} numerantur; ut ab initio \textit{Arietis} fiant oportet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L002}{}{90° ab ascensionibus tabulae demere. Cfr. t. I, p. 163–64 et Addenda. — \Name{Ptolemaeus} non-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L003}{}{nisi ascensiones rectas decadum graduum in tabulis posuit, sicut antea monui, ad pag. 58.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0671-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L004}{}{\Indent Aequationum temporis tabula apud Ptolemaeum desideratur; quo modo apud \Name{Albate-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L005}{5}{\Name{nium} aequatio illa intelligenda sit (semper nempe subtractiva) dictum est in adnotationibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L006}{}{t. I, p. 221–22.}
\par\vspace{8mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0671-H07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L007}{}{\CenterLine{\textit{Ad pag. 65-66.} — \textbf{Ascensiones obliquae pro singulis climatibus.}}}
\par\vspace{4mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0671-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L008}{}{\Indent Tabularum mentio cap. XIII et XXXV, t. I, p. 27–28, 29, 73.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0671-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L009}{}{\Indent Ab initio \textit{Arietis} numerantur, non a Capricorno ut ascensiones rectae. Differunt a \textit{Pto-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L010}{10}{\textit{lemaicis} II, 8 (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 104–108) ad graduum decades quoque supputatis, ob diver-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L011}{}{sam eclipticae obliquitatem diversasque igitur climatum latitudines. — Est quaedam proportio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L012}{}{in augmento ascensionum respondentium intervallo quadrantis horae in longitudine diei; qua}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L013}{}{re codicis mendas sanare haud difficile erat. Nonnullis locis in quibus dubitatio exstabat, cal-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L014}{}{culos duxi utens tabulis Albatenianis declinationum, ascensionum rectarum, et formula}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0671-H15}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L015}{15}{\CenterLine{sin \textit{m} = tg \textgreek{φ} tg \textgreek{δ}.}}
\par\vspace{8mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0671-H16}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L016}{}{\CenterLine{\textit{Ad pag. 67.} — \textbf{Ascensiones obliquae urbium Mekkah, Baghdād et Ḥarrān.}}}
\par\vspace{4mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0671-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L017}{}{\Indent « Tempora horaria » (\textit{azmān as-sā‘āt,} \textgreek{ὡριαῖοι χρόνοι}) sunt durationes horarum tempora-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L018}{}{lium indicatae gradibus circuli aequinoctialis, quorum 15° horam aequinoctialem conficiunt. —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L019}{}{Sequens f. 183,r. codicis vacuum erat.}
\par\vspace{8mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0671-H20}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L020}{20}{\CenterLine{\textit{Ad pag. 68-71.} — \textbf{Ascensiones obliquae urbis ar-Raqqah.}}}
\par\vspace{4mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0671-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L021}{}{\Indent Tabulae cap. XIII et XXXV, t. I, p. 27–28, 29, 73, memoratae.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0671-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L022}{}{\Indent In titulo interpolator Arabico-Africanus addidit f. 184,r.: \ArabicRun{وهي مطالع بجاية والمرية} « et hae sunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L023}{}{« ascensiones [urbium] Biǵāyah (Bougie) et Almeria », f. 184,v.–185,r.: \ArabicRun{وما حاذاها مشرق ومغرب}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L024}{}{« et eius quod illis e regione respondet [in] Oriente et Occidente »; f. 185,v.: \ArabicRun{وبجاية والمرية وما}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L025}{25}{\ArabicRun{حاذاها} « et Biǵāyah et Almeriae et eius quod illis e regione respondet ». Cfr. quae p. 218 dixi.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0671-P08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L026}{}{\Indent \textbf{\textsf{Pag. 71}}, ult. lin., tempora horaria 30° Aquarii ponantur \ArabicRun{يج لد} 13° 34′ loco \ArabicRun{يج لج} 13° 33′ co-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L027}{}{dicis et versionis.}
\par\vspace{8mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0671-H28}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L028}{}{\CenterLine{\textit{Ad pag. 72-77.} — \textbf{Medii motus Solis, Lunae, nodi ad calendarium Romanum.}}}
\par\vspace{4mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0671-P09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L029}{}{\Indent Tabulae cap. XXVII, XXXIII ac XXXVII, t. I, p. 42, 71, 75 memoratae.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0671-P10}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L030}{30}{\Indent De columnarum titulis eadem sunt dicenda quae ad similes tabulas p. 19–23 iam exposui.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L031}{}{Aera est Dhū ’l-qarnayn vel Seleucidarum, sed iuxta annum fictum a 1 \textit{ādhār} (Martii) anni}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L032}{}{\textit{praecedentis} incipientem, ne quid incommodi ex intercalatione Iuliana oriatur; cfr. t. I, p. 246.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0671-body-L033}{}{Hunc tabularum Albatenii usum ita memorat \Name{Samps Puchares} (de quo t. I, p. XXX, adn. 5),}
\DSource{0672}{223}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L001}{}{loco in \textit{Catal. codd. astrol. Graecorum,} t. I, p. 88 relato: \textgreek{Καὶ τὴν ἀρχὴν τοῦ χρόνου} (1) \textgreek{ἐκεί}-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L002}{}{\textgreek{νου} [i. e. \textgreek{Ἀλεξάνδρου τοῦ υἱοῦ Φιλίππου, οὗτος Δουλκαρνάην ὀνομάζεται}] \textgreek{τινὲς ἀπὸ τοῦ ͵}ʹ \textgreek{κρατοῦσι}·}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L003}{}{\textgreek{τὸ ὄνομα δὲ τοῦ μηνὸς ἐκείνων Ἀδάρ, διὰ τοῦτο ὅτι ἡ ἀρχὴ τοῦ κόσμου εἰς τοῦτον τὸν μῆνα ἀνα}-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L004}{}{\textgreek{καινίζεται ἤτοι λαμβάνει ἀρχήν}. \textgreek{διὰ τοῦτο καὶ ὁ Πατανῆς} [= al-Battānī] \textgreek{τὴν ὁδὸν τῶν ἀστέρων εἰς}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L005}{5}{\textgreek{τὴν σύνταξιν αὐτοῦ ἐν τούτῳ τῷ ἔτει} (2) \textgreek{τέϑεικε, τὴν ἀρχὴν δὲ τοῦ ἔτους αὐτοῦ ἀπὸ τοῦ Ἀδὰρ ἔθηκε}.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0672-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L006}{}{\Indent De quantitate motus Lunae cfr. t. I, p. 225; de epocha fundamentali v. quae \Name{Schiapa-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L007}{}{\Name{relli}, t. I, p. 246 dixit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0672-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L008}{}{\Indent \textbf{\textsf{Pag. 74}}. Codex \textit{subāṭ} (Februarium) dumtaxat communium annorum indicat; placuit su-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L009}{}{bāṭ etiam bisextilium addere, ut p. 21 pro dhū ’l-ḥiǵǵah feceram (cfr. adn. p. 204).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0672-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L010}{10}{\Indent \textbf{\textsf{Pag. 75–76}}. Cfr. quae de aequalibus tabulis p. 22–23, in adnotationibus p. 204 dixi.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0672-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L011}{}{\Indent \textbf{\textsf{Pag. 77}}. Motus 20 annorum in cod. omissi, forte quia in titulis (excepto motu nodi) in-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L012}{}{dicati.}
\par\vspace{6mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0672-H13}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L013}{}{\CenterLine{\textit{Ad pag. 78-83.} — \textbf{Aequationes Solis et Lunae, latitudo Lunae.}}}
\par\vspace{4mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0672-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L014}{}{\Indent Tabulae aequationum cap. XXVII, XXX, XXXIII, XXXVI, XLII (t. I, p. 47, 51, 52, 53–}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L015}{15}{-54, 72, 75, 93) memorantur; latitudinis cap. XXXVIII ac XLIII (t. I, p. 76 et 97).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0672-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L016}{}{\Indent Col. II: \textit{Aequatio Solis.} — A tabula \Name{Ptolemaei} III, 5 (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 201) differt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L017}{}{ob diversam aequationis maximae quantitatem, de qua t. I, p. 213–14 dixi. Ptolemaeus prae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L018}{}{terea tabulae argumentum (anomaliam mediam) intervallo 6 graduum in primo et quarto qua-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L019}{}{drante, 3 graduum in secundo ac tertio, exhibet; in aequatione secundas omittit. — In tabula}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L020}{20}{\textit{Albateniana,} cuius argumentum per singulos gradus procedit, intervalla in aequatione Solis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L021}{}{satis sunt regularia ut numeri codicis statim emendari possint; raro mihi opus fuit calculos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L022}{}{ducere formula tg aequat. = $\dfrac{e_1 \sin m}{1 + e_1 \cos m}$, ubi $e_1$ est epicycli radius aut excentricitas orbitae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L023}{}{in partibus decimalibus radii orbitae = 1, i. e. Albatenianum $\dfrac{e}{60}$. Cfr. t. I, p. 48 et 219–20.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0672-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L024}{}{\Indent Col. III–VI: \textit{Inaequalitas motus lunaris longitudinis.} — Nonnulla praemittenda sunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L025}{25}{ut clarius tabularum constructio et usus intelligantur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0672-P08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L026}{}{\Indent In hypothesi Ptolemaica, quam breviter \Name{al-Battānī} cap. XXX (t. I, p. 50–54) exponit,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L027}{}{motus lunares in plano circuli \textit{deflectentis,} seu \textit{obliqui} respectu zodiaci, fiunt. \textit{Nodi,} seu in-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L028}{}{tersectiones obliqui et parecliptici, circulum pareclipticum motu retrogrado ambiunt 3 minu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L029}{}{nutorum circiter in die.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0672-P09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L030}{30}{\Indent Luna in circumferentia movetur \textit{epicycli} (3), cuius radius 5\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 15′ partium est qualium ra-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L031}{}{dius obliqui 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}. Si centrum epicycli circumferentiam obliqui percurreret, inaequalitas motus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L032}{}{Lunae una esset, ut inaequalitas motus Solis. At centrum epicycli lunaris ambit circumferen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L033}{}{tiam excentrici (deferentis), qui in uno puncto, scilicet in apogeo A excentrici, circulum obli-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L034}{}{quum tangit; qua re primae inaequalitati (cuius correctio vocatur \textit{aequatio simplex}) semper}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L035}{35}{altera adiungitur quotiescumque centrum epicycli seu Luna ab apogeo excentrici recedat. Ex-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L036}{}{centricitas est 10\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 19′ partium qualium radius obliqui 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}; itaque radius excentrici 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L037}{}{— 10\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 19′ = 49\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 41′. Et quoniam apogeum excentrici particeps est motus retrogradi obliqui,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L038}{}{centrum excentrici circellum describit circa centrum Terrae (vel zodiaci, vel parecliptici) cu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-body-L039}{}{ius radius est ipsa excentricitas.}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0672-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) \textgreek{Χρόνος} apud Byzantinos scriptores \textit{annum}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-notes_left-L002}{}{significat.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0672-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-notes_left-L003}{}{\Indent (2) \textgreek{Ἔτος} est « \textit{annorum periodus} ».}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0672-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-notes_right-L001}{}{\Indent (3) Superne in praecedentia (ab ortu ad occa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-notes_right-L002}{}{sum), inferne in consequentia signorum (ab occasu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0672-notes_right-L003}{}{ad ortum).}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0673}{224}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0673-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L001}{}{\Indent Praeterea apogeum epicycli distinguitur in \textit{verum} (\textgreek{τὸ ἀκριβὲς ἀπόγειον}) et \textit{medium} (\textgreek{τὸ}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L002}{}{\textgreek{μέσον ἀπόγειον}). Verum, seu G (in circello cuius centrum M), est epicycli punctum in quod}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L003}{}{incidit linea recta ex centro Terrae per centrum epicycli ducta; medium (seu H eiusdem cir-}
\par\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.555\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0673-body-F01}{}\hspace*{18mm}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0673-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.425\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.575\FullSourceWidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L004}{}{celli), a quo anomalia media numeratur,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L005}{5}{epicycli punctum in quod cadit linea recta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L006}{}{per centrum epicycli ducta ex puncto F′ li-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L007}{}{neae apsidalis excentrici, quod a centro E}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L008}{}{Terrae tanto distat quanto, in opposita parte,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L009}{}{centrum excentrici ab eodem puncto E. Dif-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L010}{10}{ferentia apogeorum veri et medii, seu arcus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L011}{}{GH, a \Name{Ptolemaeo} \textgreek{ἡ πρόσνευσις τοῦ τῆς}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L012}{}{\textgreek{σελήνης ἐπικύκλου} « adsensus epicycli Lunae »}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L013}{}{vocatur; maxima (13° 9′) in 60° et 120° elon-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L014}{}{gationis Lunae a Sole, nulla in syzygiis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L015}{15}{(i. e. Luna in apogeo excentrici consistente)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L016}{}{et in quadraturis (seu Luna perigeum ex-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L017}{}{centrici occupante). Ob hanc prosneusim \textit{p,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L018}{}{aequatio simplex Lunae non pendet, ut So-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L019}{}{lis, ab anomalia media \textit{m,} sed ab anomalia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L020}{20}{correcta \textit{m} + \textit{p}; qua re, si \textit{r} vocemus ra-}
\end{minipage}\par
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L021}{}{dium epicycli in partibus radii obliqui 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} partium, aequatio simplex Lunae supputabitur for-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L022}{}{mula (cfr. t. I, p. 219–20)}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0673-H23}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L023}{}{\CenterLine{tang aequat. simplicis = $R\,\dfrac{r \sin (m + p)}{60^{p}\,R + r \cos (m + p)}$\,,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L024}{}{quam nos, ponendo $r_1 = \dfrac{r}{60}$ (i. e. radium epicycli in partibus decimalibus radii obliqui = 1),}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L025}{25}{et radium \textit{R,} ad quem functiones trigonometricae referuntur, 1 partis, scribemus}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0673-H26}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L026}{}{\CenterLine{tang aequat. simplicis = $\dfrac{r_1 \sin (m + p)}{1 + r_1 \cos (m + p)}$\,.}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0673-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L027}{}{\Indent Nunc \textit{p} supputemus. Angulus AEM seu FEM, quem linea apsidum excentrici cum linea}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L028}{}{apsidum epicycli efficit, demonstratur semper aequari duplae elongationi Lunae mediae a Sole}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L029}{}{medio, nempe 2 (\MoonSym{} — \SunSym{}) esse. Sit \textgreek{ρ} distantia variabilis Lunae seu centri epicycli a Terra,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L030}{30}{\textit{e} excentricitas in partibus quarum 60 radio obliqui tribuuntur; ut ipse \Name{al-Battānī} ca-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L031}{}{pite XXXIX docet, erit}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0673-H32}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L032}{}{\CenterLine{$\rho = \sqrt{(60^{p} - e)^2 - \left(\dfrac{e \sin 2\,(\MoonSym{} - \SunSym{})}{R}\right)^{2}} \pm \dfrac{e \cos 2\,(\MoonSym{} - \SunSym{})}{R}$\,,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L033}{}{vel, sumptis \textit{R} et radio obliqui = 1\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}, et excentricitate $e_1$ in eius partibus:}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0673-H34}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L034}{}{\CenterLine{$\rho = \sqrt{(1 - e_1)^2 - e_1^{\,2} \sin^2 2\,(\MoonSym{} - \SunSym{})} \pm e_1 \cos 2\,(\MoonSym{} - \SunSym{})$.}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0673-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L035}{35}{\Indent In figura est ME = \textgreek{ρ}, arcus HG = ang. HMG = ang. EMF′ = \textit{p}; considerando trian-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L036}{}{gulo MEF′ (ducta perpendiculari ex F′ ad latus ME) statim deducitur}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0673-H37}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L037}{}{\CenterLine{tang $p$ = tang $EMF'$ = $R\,\dfrac{EF' \sin F'EM}{R \times EM - EF' \cos F'EM} = R\,\dfrac{EF' \sin FEM}{R \times EM + EF' \cos FEM}$}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0673-H38}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0673-body-L038}{}{\CenterLine{tang $p$ = $R\,\dfrac{e \sin 2\,(\MoonSym{} - \SunSym{})}{R\rho + e \cos 2\,(\MoonSym{} - \SunSym{})} = R\,\dfrac{\left(\dfrac{e}{\rho}\right) \sin 2\,(\MoonSym{} - \SunSym{})}{R + \left(\dfrac{e}{\rho}\right) \cos 2\,(\MoonSym{} - \SunSym{})}$}}
\DSource{0674}{225}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L001}{}{vel, pro \textit{e} sumpto $e_1$, $R$ = 1 facto, \textgreek{ρ} in partibus decimalibus delecto ($\rho_1$):}
\par\vspace{1mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0674-H02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L002}{}{\CenterLine{tg $p = \dfrac{e_1 \sin 2\,(\MoonSym{} - \SunSym{})}{\rho_1 + \varepsilon_1 \cos 2\,(\MoonSym{} - \SunSym{})} = \dfrac{\left(\dfrac{e_1}{\rho_1}\right) \sin 2\,(\MoonSym{} - \SunSym{})}{1 + \left(\dfrac{e_1}{\rho_1}\right) \cos 2\,(\MoonSym{} - \SunSym{})}$\,.}}
\par\vspace{1mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L003}{}{Ergo prosneusis \textit{p} est functio duplae elongationis. In IV columna, cuius argumentum du-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L004}{}{pla elongatio media, reperitur et ab \Name{al-Battānī} optime \textit{aequatio anomaliae} vocatur (1).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0674-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L005}{5}{\Indent \textit{Secunda inaequalitas} ab excentrico epicyclum deferenti provenit, ob accessum centri}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L006}{}{epicycli Lunae ad Terram, dum ab apogeo excentrici recedit duplo motu elongationis Lunae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L007}{}{a Sole; semper, ut dixi, primae inaequalitati additur extra syzygias i. e. quotiescumque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L008}{}{centrum epicycli (seu Luna) ab apogeo excentrici divellitur. Quando Luna est in quadraturis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L009}{}{(\textgreek{διχοτομίαι}) mediis, scilicet perigeum excentrici occupat, distantia \textgreek{ρ} non 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} sed 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} — 2\textit{e} =}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L010}{10}{= 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} — 20\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 38′ = 39\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 22′ fit; qua re \textit{r} formulae aequationis simplicis tunc ad radium 39\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 22′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L011}{}{refertur, et non $\dfrac{5^{p}\,15'}{60^{p}}$ sed $\dfrac{5^{p}\,15'}{39^{p}\,22'}$ vel $\dfrac{8^{p}}{60^{p}}$ est. Calculis ita ductis, aequatio perigea Lunae in-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L012}{}{venitur 7° 40′, hoc est excedere aequationem simplicem apogeam (5° 1′) quantitate 2° 39′. Haec}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L013}{}{differentia « \textit{secunda inaequalitas} » vocatur, cuius dimidiam partem hodie astronomi Euro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L014}{}{paei \textit{evectionem} appellant; in VI columna tabulae exhibetur pro singulis anomaliae aequa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L015}{15}{tae gradibus.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0674-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L016}{}{\Indent Similiter in distantiis intermediis (cognita \textgreek{ρ} ex dupla elongatione Lunae a Sole) suppu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L017}{}{tatur quanto debeat aequatio simplex (vel apogea) augeri. Portiones pro singulis distantiis in-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L018}{}{termediis addendas \Name{Ptolemaeus} V, 7 (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 313–15), ut simpliciorem tabu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L019}{}{lam conderet, ad maximam differentiam 2° 39′ retulit; hoc est in tabula indicavit quota pars,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L020}{20}{pro distantiis respondentibus duplae elongationi datae, sumi debeat ex secunda inaequalitate,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L021}{}{cum haec in unitatem (1° = 60′) conversa sit. Tales \textit{sexagesimae partes proportionales} in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L022}{}{V columna, cuius argumentum dupla elongatio media, indicantur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0674-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L023}{}{\Indent Ergo operationes ad supputandam \textit{aequationem totam} seu \textit{compositam} Lunae hae sunt:}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0674-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L024}{}{\Indent\Indent 1\textsuperscript{o}. Ex tabulis mediorum motuum sumantur longitudo Lunae media, longitudo media}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L025}{25}{Solis, anomalia media Lunae; duplicata differentia longitudinum erit dupla elongatio media.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0674-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L026}{}{\Indent\Indent 2\textsuperscript{o}. Cum dupla elongatione media in tabulam intrando, sumatur ex IV columna cor-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L027}{}{rectio anomaliae mediae, ex V fractio sexagesima; correctio addatur anomaliae in primo et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L028}{}{quarto quadrante, subtrahatur in secundo et tertio.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0674-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L029}{}{\Indent\Indent 3\textsuperscript{o}. Cum anomalia aequata seu correcta intrando, sumantur aequatio simplex vel apo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L030}{30}{gea ex III col., et « minima longinquitas » (sive correctio in perigeo afferenda aequationi apo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L031}{}{geae) ex VI.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0674-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L032}{}{\Indent\Indent 4\textsuperscript{o}. Sit \textit{c} correctio e VI col. sumpta, \textit{f} fractio sexagesima ex V; correctio aequationis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L033}{}{simplicis in III col. repertae erit $\dfrac{c \times f}{60'}$. Hoc est de \textit{c} pars proportionalis ad minuta \textit{f} sumatur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0674-P08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L034}{}{\Indent\Indent 5\textsuperscript{o}. Aequatio simplex + $\dfrac{c \times f}{60'}$ = aequatio centri correcta; quae longitudini mediae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L035}{35}{subtrahitur vel additur, prout longitudo minor vel maior 180 gradibus est. Proveniet \textit{longi-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-body-L036}{}{\textit{tudo vera} Lunae quaesita.}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0674-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) Haec prosneusis est dimidia fere pars ter-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-notes_left-L002}{}{tiae inaequalitatis lunaris a \Name{Tychone Brahe} in-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-notes_left-L003}{}{ventae et \textit{variationis} nomine notae; praeter quae}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-notes_right-L001}{}{t. I, p. 224 dixi, cfr. I. \Name{Bullialdi}, \textit{Astronomia}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0674-notes_right-L002}{}{\textit{philolaica,} Parisiis 1645, p. 173.}
\end{minipage}
\par\vspace{5mm}\hfill{\fontsize{8}{10}\selectfont 29}\hspace{10mm}
\DSource{0675}{226}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0675-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L001}{}{\Indent Tabulae Albatenii nituntur \textit{Ptolemaica} V, 7 (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 316–17), quae inscri-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L002}{}{bitur \textgreek{κανόνιον τῆς καϑόλου σεληνιακῆς ἀνωμαλίας} « tabella universae inaequalitatis lunaris »; at}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L003}{}{non sine bonis mutationibus. Argumentum tabulae apud \Name{Ptolemaeum} per senos gradus in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L004}{}{primo et quarto quadrante, per ternos in secundo et tertio, collectum, apud \Name{al-Battānī} per}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L005}{5}{singulos gradus procedit. — Aequationem III col. (\textgreek{πλάτους καὶ μήκους προσϑαφαιρέσεις ἐπικύκλου}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L006}{}{« prosthaphaereses latitudinis et longitudinis epicycli ») \Name{Ptolemaeus} absque secundis et ru-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L007}{}{diore modo supputavit (1), qua re apud eum errores saepe 1′ (semel 1′ 9″) attingunt, ut e}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L008}{}{tabella apud \Name{Delambre}, \textit{Hist. de l’astron. ancienne,} t. II, p. 176 apparet (2). Longe me-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L009}{}{lius \Name{al-Battānī}, qui secundas etiam supputavit. Ut codicis mendas sanarem, logarithmis sep-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L010}{10}{tem decimalium et formula\quad tg aeq. = $\dfrac{e \sin m}{1 + \cos m}$\quad $\left(\text{ubi } e = \dfrac{5^{p}\,15'}{60^{p}}\right)$ pro numeris multiplis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L011}{}{6 denuo calculos institui; reliqua, praeter paucos casus in quibus logarithmos adhibui, facili}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L012}{}{interpolatione probavi. Pag. 79 tres errores emendare oportet:}
\par\vspace{2mm}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L016}{}{Saepe secundae etiam apud \Name{al-Battānī} cum numeris per logarithmos repertis ad amussim}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L017}{}{congruunt; interdum discrepantiae 1″, 2″, 3″ occurrunt, perraro maiores (5° 0′ 57″ pro 5° 1′ 9″}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L018}{}{sub 96°; 4° 53′ 30″ pro 4° 53′ 19″ sub 108°; 4° 52′ 10″ pro 4° 52′ 3″ sub 109°; 4° 44′ 6″}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L019}{}{pro 4° 44′ 17″ sub 114°).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0675-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L020}{20}{\Indent In IV col. (\textgreek{ἐκκέντρου προσϑαφαιρέσεις ἀπογείου} « prosthaphaereses apogei excentrici ») \Name{al-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L021}{}{\Name{Battānī} numeros Ptolemaicos omnes recepit (tantum sub 108 Ptol. 13° 3′, Alb. 13° 2′), et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L022}{}{reliquos interpolatione deduxit. « Cette colonne n’est juste qu’à une ou deux minutes près, ce}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L023}{}{« qui est suffisant », affirmat \Name{Delambre} (apud Halma, notes, I, 36). — Cod. in his tabulis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L024}{}{Lunae \ArabicRun{الحصة} \textit{al-ḥiṣṣah} semper pro \ArabicRun{الحاصة} \textit{al-ḥāṣṣah} exhibet; sed \ArabicRun{الحاصة} restituere non dubitavi,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L025}{25}{ut al-Battānī in reliquis tabulis et in capitibus habet. Error ab amanuensi provenit, qui usum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L026}{}{quorundam scriptorum \textit{Arabo-Africanorum} secutus est. Ex hoc usu Arabum occidentalium,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L027}{}{per versiones \Name{Gherardi Cremonensis} et \textit{Tabulas Alphonsinas,} apud multos nostra-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L028}{}{tes XVII saeculi etiam voces \textit{Argumentum verum, Argumentum medium, Aequatio ar-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L029}{}{\textit{gumenti} pro anomalia vera, anomalia media, aequatione anomaliae (Lunae) irrepserunt; cfr.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L030}{30}{definitiones apud I. B. \Name{Riccioli}, \textit{Almagestum Novum,} Bononiae 1651, t. I, p. 259.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0675-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L031}{}{\Indent In V col. (\textgreek{διάφορα ἑξηκοστῶν} « differentiae partium sexagesimarum ») secundas omittit, quas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L032}{}{\Name{Ptolemaeus} habet.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0675-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L033}{}{\Indent In VI col. (\textgreek{ἐπικύκλου διάφορα} « differentiae epicycli ») est quantitas qua aequatio perigea}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L034}{}{apogeam excedit. \Name{Al-Battānī} numeros \Name{Ptolemaei} recepit (3); reliquos interpolavit. De}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L035}{35}{Ptolemaicis ita \Name{Delambre}, \textit{Astr. ancienne} II, 200 iudicat: « J’en ai calculé quelques exem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-body-L036}{}{« ples qui m’ont prouvé que les quantités (équat. périgée–équat. apogée) ou différences de}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0675-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) Eadem aequationis simplicis tabula (\textgreek{κανό}-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-notes_left-L002}{}{\textgreek{νιον τῆς πρώτης καὶ ἁπλῆς ἀνωμαλίας τῆς σελήνης})}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-notes_left-L003}{}{etiam IV, 9 (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 273).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0675-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-notes_left-L004}{}{\Indent (2) In hac \Name{Delambrii} tabella haec emenden-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-notes_left-L005}{}{tur: sub 78, 84, 114 aequationes sunt 4° \textit{48}′ 18″}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-notes_right-L001}{}{(Del. 8′), 4° 55′ \textit{43}″ (Del. 39″), 4° 44′ \textit{17}″ (Del. 23″).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0675-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-notes_right-L002}{}{\Indent (3) Interdum ± 1′ discrepantiae, quam mani-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-notes_right-L003}{}{feste Albatenius ipse voluit; maxima quantitas 2° 40′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0675-notes_right-L004}{}{pro Ptolemaei 2° 39′.}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0676-body-L001}{}{« l’épicycle ne sont quelquefois exactes qu’à 2′ près. Plus souvent l’erreur est de 1′ ou moin-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0676-body-L002}{}{« dre encore ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0676-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0676-body-L003}{}{\Indent Col. VII: \textit{Latitudo Lunae.} — Tabula \Name{Ptolemaei} quoque est cum tabula inaequali-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0676-body-L004}{}{tatum lunarium collecta, qua re apud eum argumentum (motus latitudinis) non per singulos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0676-body-L005}{5}{sed per senos aut ternos gradus procedit. \Name{Ptolemaeus} praeterea secunda omittit; argu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0676-body-L006}{}{mentum autem a \textit{limite boreali} (\textgreek{βόρειον πέρας}) numerat, non a nodo ascendenti, qua re in ar-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0676-body-L007}{}{gumento 0° Ptol. = 90° Albatenii (cfr. t. I, p. 250–51). — Tabulam suam \Name{al-Battānī} for-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0676-body-L008}{}{mula\quad sin lat. = $\dfrac{\text{sin argum. lat.} \times \text{sin maximae latit.}}{R}$\quad supputavit; eandem tabulis logarithmicis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0676-body-L009}{}{\Name{Schiaparelli} probavit.}
\par\vspace{8mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0676-H10}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0676-body-L010}{10}{\CenterLine{\textit{Ad pag. 84–87.} — \textbf{Syzygiae mediae ad annos mensesque Romanos.}}}
\par\vspace{4mm}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0676-body-L011}{}{\Indent Tabulae t. I, p. 92, cap. XLII memoratae.}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0676-body-L012}{}{\Indent Antea, p. 29–32, syzygias medias ad annos vagos seu Aegyptios exhibuerat, nunc ad an-}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0676-body-L014}{}{antea p. 222–23 et t. I, p. 246 dixi, in tabulis suis al-Battānī \textit{ficto anno} Romano uti, cuius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0676-body-L015}{15}{initium 1 ādhār (Martii) anni \textit{chronologici iam incepti;} ergo menses a Septembri ad Fe-}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0678-body-L030}{30}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{30.10mm}\smash{\llap{52}\rlap{.5}}}\rlap{\hspace*{35.60mm}\smash{\rlap{minutis}}}\rlap{\hspace*{69.20mm}\smash{\llap{0}}}\rlap{\hspace*{77.10mm}\smash{\llap{2}}}\rlap{\hspace*{84.60mm}\smash{\llap{9}}}\rlap{\hspace*{106.10mm}\smash{\llap{0}}}\rlap{\hspace*{114.20mm}\smash{\llap{28}}}\rlap{\hspace*{121.50mm}\smash{\llap{34}\rlap{. 6}}}\rlap{\hspace*{146.50mm}\smash{\llap{0}}}\rlap{\hspace*{154.80mm}\smash{\llap{0}}}\rlap{\hspace*{162.30mm}\smash{\llap{6}\rlap{. 8}}}}
\par\nointerlineskip\vspace{1pt}\noindent\rlap{\hspace*{59.30mm}\rule{28.50mm}{.4pt}}\rlap{\hspace*{99.40mm}\rule{28.00mm}{.4pt}}\rlap{\hspace*{138.80mm}\rule{28.10mm}{.4pt}}\par\nointerlineskip\vspace{2pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0678-body-L031}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{69.20mm}\smash{\llap{6}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{77.10mm}\smash{\llap{37}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{84.60mm}\smash{\llap{23}\rlap{″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{106.10mm}\smash{\llap{153}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{114.20mm}\smash{\llap{17}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{121.50mm}\smash{\llap{38}\rlap{″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{146.50mm}\smash{\llap{25}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{154.80mm}\smash{\llap{58}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{162.30mm}\smash{\llap{27}\rlap{″}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0678-body-L032}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{132.90mm}\smash{\rlap{+}}}\rlap{\hspace*{146.50mm}\smash{\llap{6}}}\rlap{\hspace*{154.80mm}\smash{\llap{37}}}\rlap{\hspace*{162.30mm}\smash{\llap{23}}}}
\par\nointerlineskip\vspace{1pt}\noindent\rlap{\hspace*{138.80mm}\rule{28.10mm}{.4pt}}\par\nointerlineskip\vspace{2pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0678-body-L033}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{146.50mm}\smash{\llap{32}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{154.80mm}\smash{\llap{35}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{162.30mm}\smash{\llap{50}\rlap{″}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0678-body-L034}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{20.30mm}\smash{\rlap{al-Battānī}}}\rlap{\hspace*{69.20mm}\smash{\llap{6}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{77.10mm}\smash{\llap{36}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{84.60mm}\smash{\llap{55}\rlap{″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{106.10mm}\smash{\llap{153}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{114.20mm}\smash{\llap{11}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{121.50mm}\smash{\llap{7}\rlap{″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{146.50mm}\smash{\llap{32}}}\rlap{\hspace*{154.80mm}\smash{\llap{35}}}\rlap{\hspace*{162.30mm}\smash{\llap{56}}}}
\par\nointerlineskip\vspace{1pt}\noindent\rlap{\hspace*{59.30mm}\rule{28.50mm}{.4pt}}\rlap{\hspace*{99.40mm}\rule{28.00mm}{.4pt}}\rlap{\hspace*{138.80mm}\rule{28.10mm}{.4pt}}\par\nointerlineskip\vspace{2pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0678-body-L035}{35}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{39.20mm}\smash{\rlap{differentia}}}\rlap{\hspace*{84.60mm}\smash{\llap{— 28}\rlap{″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{114.20mm}\smash{\llap{— 6}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{121.50mm}\smash{\llap{31}\rlap{″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{162.30mm}\smash{\llap{+ 6}\rlap{″}}}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0678-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0678-body-L036}{}{\Indent Harum omnium discrepantiarum causam recte non intelligo.}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0678-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0678-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) Tabulae mediorum motuum non praebent}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0678-notes_left-L002}{}{motum latitudinis Lunae sed motum nodi retrogra-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0678-notes_left-L003}{}{dum; cui si motus longitudinis addatur, exibit motus}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0678-notes_right-L001}{}{latitudinis (\textgreek{ἡ κατὰ πλάτος κίνησις}). Manifeste autem in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0678-notes_right-L002}{}{coniunctionibus longitudo Lunae a longitudine Solis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0678-notes_right-L003}{}{non differt.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0679}{230}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0679-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L001}{}{\Indent \textbf{\textsf{Pag. 84–85}}, pro \textit{annis collectis.} Data prima numerorum linea, reliqui numeri inveniun-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L002}{}{tur semper addendo 4\textsuperscript{\textit{d}} 35′ 11″ in columna dierum, et motus medios huic temporis intervallo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L003}{}{respondentes in columnis reliquis; at si dierum summa lunationem integram (29\textsuperscript{\textit{d}} 31′ 50″) ex-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L004}{}{cedit, novilunium repertum erit novilunium secundum mensis ādhār aut primum mensis se-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L005}{5}{quentis, qua re de summa erit lunatio integra demenda ac de motibus reliquarum columnarum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L006}{}{motus lunationi respondentes.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0679-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L007}{}{\Indent \textbf{\textsf{Pag. 86}}, pro \textit{annis singulis.} Sit A temporis momentum quo primum novilunium ādhār}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L008}{}{contigit anno quocumque; anno sequenti novilunium ādhār erit A + 18\textsuperscript{\textit{d}} 53′ 52″ si annus sequens}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L009}{}{communis fuerit, aut A — 1\textsuperscript{\textit{d}} + 18\textsuperscript{\textit{d}} 53′ 52″ si annus sequens bisextilis; ut semper, quando}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L010}{10}{summa spatium lunationis (29\textsuperscript{\textit{d}} 31′ 50″) excedit debent lunatio motusque ei respondentes demi. —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L011}{}{\textbf{[}Pro anno 11 codex praebet 28\textsuperscript{\textit{d}} 9′ 39″, 358° 32′ 6″, 271° 4′ 19″, 211° 11′ 43″; pro anno 22}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L012}{}{codex 26\textsuperscript{\textit{d}} 19′ 18″, 357° 4′ 18″, 182° 8′ 39″, 62° 23′ 27″. Hos numeros ex tabula p. 32 ineptus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L013}{}{\Name{interpolator} sumpsit, quid discriminis inter tabulam pro annis vagis et tabulam pro annis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L014}{}{Iulianis intercederet non intelligens. Nam, ob bisextiles, dies integri secundae columnae in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L015}{15}{tabula annorum Romanorum quarto quoque anno diem amittunt respectu tabulae annorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L016}{}{Aegyptiorum; hoc est initium mensis quod primo anno in ambabus tabulis unum idemque erat,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L017}{}{quarto quoque anno differentiam diei exhibet, post octo annos duorum dierum, post duodecim}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L018}{}{annos trium etc. Potest igitur fieri ut novilunium thōth tabulae Aegyptiae in mensem ādhār}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L019}{}{tabulae Romanae non cadat, sed in mensem subāṭ (Februarium). Quod undecimo tabularum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L020}{20}{anno evenit. In tabulis Aegyptiis, adiecta quantitate 18\textsuperscript{\textit{d}} 53′ 52″ ad 11\textsuperscript{\textit{d}} 47′ 37″, pro novilunio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L021}{}{thōth 30\textsuperscript{\textit{d}} 41′ 29″ exeunt, manifeste dies thōth excedentia; dempta igitur lunatione novilunium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L022}{}{relinquitur thōth 1\textsuperscript{\textit{d}} 9′ 39″ (29″ est typothetae menda). Sed in calendario Romano hoc novi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L023}{}{lunium ad subāṭ (Februarium) pertinet; nam ad 9\textsuperscript{\textit{d}} 47′ 37″ tabulae Romanae addenda sunt,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L024}{}{ob sequentem bisextilem, 17\textsuperscript{\textit{d}} 53′ 52″, unde ādhār 28\textsuperscript{\textit{d}} 41′ 29″ oritur, a quo nihil detrahen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L025}{25}{dum. — Similiter anno 22 res se habet. Hae differentiae semper frequentiores fierent si ta-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L026}{}{bulae ultra 24 annos producerentur; post 120 annos thōth e mense ādhār seu Martio (cum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L027}{}{quo ab initio communia primum diem et novilunium habebat) omnino exiret, qua re Lunae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L028}{}{mensis ādhār fierent omnes a Lunis thōth diversae. Haec non animadvertens, \Name{interpolator}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L029}{}{tabulam Romanam ad fidem Aegyptiae et Ptolemaei emendandam falso putaverat (1), licet dies}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L030}{30}{integros secundae columnae quales ob bisextiles oriebantur servavisset. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.\textbf{]}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0679-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L031}{}{\Indent In secundis dierum anno 6 respondentium pro 40″, ut impressum est, restituatur 50″. —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L032}{}{Secundae in III columna nonnihilo (2″ circiter) discrepant a secundis p. 32; \Name{al-Battānī} hanc}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L033}{}{columnam ex novo supputasse apparet, motu diurno elongationis perpaulo diverso fortasse sumpto.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0679-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L034}{}{\Indent \textbf{\textsf{Pag. 87}}, pro \textit{mensibus} etc. Cfr. quae p. 206 de similibus tribus tabulis (p. 31) exposita}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L035}{35}{sunt. In tertia tabella, quae multis mendis in cod. scatet (praesertim quod ultimam columnam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L036}{}{attinet) hi lapsus emendandi sunt, ut me \Name{Schiaparelli} monet:}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0679-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L037}{}{\Indent\Indent 2\textsuperscript{o} numero ultimae columnae l. 52″ pro 54″ (cod. \ArabicRun{و} 6″!)}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0679-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L038}{}{\Indent\Indent 5\textsuperscript{o} numero IV col. l. 184° \ArabicRun{قفد} pro 189° \ArabicRun{قفط}; ultimae col. 51′ pro 56′ (cod. \ArabicRun{يح} 18′!)}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0679-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L039}{}{\Indent\Indent 6\textsuperscript{o} numero ultimae col. l. 275° 48′ 22″ pro 306° 28′ 36″ (cod. 275° 4′ 10″)}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0679-P08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-body-L040}{40}{\Indent\Indent 7\textsuperscript{o} numero ultimae col. l. 321° 45′ 17″ pro 23° 5′ 45″ (cod. 291° 0′ 40″!).}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}{\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0679-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0679-notes-L001}{}{\Indent (1) Versio Hispanica f. 56, r. hoc interpolatum exemplar sequitur.}
}
\DSource{0680}{231}
\par\vspace{4mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0680-H01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L001}{}{\CenterLine{\textit{Ad pag. 88-91.} — \textbf{Tabulae ad eclipses supputandas.}}}
\par\vspace{3mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0680-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L002}{}{\Indent \textbf{\textsf{Pag. 88}}, tabella \textit{inaequalium motuum.} Memoratur cap. XLII, t. I, p. 94; apud \Name{Pto-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L003}{}{\Name{lemaeum} desideratur. — De minori tabella in dextera parte paginae, cuius mentio t. I, p. 94}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L004}{}{et 150 occurrit, vide quae \Name{Schiaparelli} t. I, p. 273, adn. \textit{a}, exposuit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0680-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L005}{5}{\Indent \textit{Termini eclipsium} Solis emendentur sicut p. 209 docui, nempe: 159° 44′ — \textit{190° 56′},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L006}{}{0° 0′ — 20° 16′, \textit{349° 4′} — 360°.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0680-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L007}{}{\Indent \textbf{\textsf{Pag. 89}}, tabula \textit{correctionis.} Memorata cap. XXXIX, XLI, XLIII, XLIV (t. I, p. 80,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L008}{}{83, 87, 88, 97, 101, 106, 110–11). Est \textgreek{κανόνιον διορϑώσεως} \Name{Ptolemaei} VI, 8 (ed. Halma,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L009}{}{t. I, p. 430), qua tamen nonnisi \textgreek{διαφόρων ἑξηκοστά} « sexagesimae partes differentiarum » exhi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L010}{10}{bentur. Columnas omnes praebet \Name{Theon}, \textit{Tabulae manuales,} t. I, p. 146. Sub numero 18,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L011}{}{pro 1′ 22″ restituendum 1′ 42″. — « Portiones longinquitatum Lunae » sunt partes propor-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L012}{}{tionales ex variatione diametri Lunae (aut Solis) inter apogeam distantiam ac perigeam su-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L013}{}{mendae, ut cognoscatur diametrus respondens anomaliae aequatae Lunae cum qua in tabulam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L014}{}{intrandum. Praeterea t. I, p. 80, anomalia Solis tamquam argumento sumpta, portiones ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L015}{15}{statuendam variationem parallaxis Solis inserviunt. — Duae reliquae columnae, a codicis scrip-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L016}{}{tore perperam in unam conflatae, correctionem parallaxi Lunari latitudinis et longitudinis af-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L017}{}{ferendam ob epicyclum et ob excentricum docent; argumentum columnae epicycli est anoma-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L018}{}{lia aequata Lunae, excentrici est dupla elongatio media. — Omnes columnae in \textit{Tabulis Al-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L019}{}{\textit{phonsinis} occurrunt, p. 116, r. editionis t. I, p. \textsc{xxxvi}, adn. 7, commemoratae; titulus ibi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L020}{20}{est: « Tabula aequationis diversitatis aspectus [i. e. parallaxis] sive tabula \textit{Attacium} ». Re-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L021}{}{stituendum « \textit{attacuim} » i. e. \textit{at-taqwīm,} quo nomine \Name{al-Battānī} etiam hanc correctionem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L022}{}{designat (1).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0680-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L023}{}{\Indent \textbf{\textsf{Pag. 89}}, \textit{digiti eclipsati.} Tres columnae ad eorum computationem inservientes memoran-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L024}{}{tur cap. XLIII et XLIV (t. I, p. 101 et 111–12); occurrunt apud \Name{Ptolemaeum} VI, 8}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L025}{25}{(ed. Halma, t. I, p. 430) ac \Name{Theonem} II, 95. In tertia columna, l. 3, Ptolemaeus 2 $\qfrac{1}{15}$}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L026}{}{(= 2\textsuperscript{\textit{dig}} 4′), Theon 2 $\qfrac{1}{5}$; l. 9, perperam in edit. Halma, 9\textsuperscript{\textit{dig}} 10′ (\textgreek{ϛ} ″) pro 9\textsuperscript{\textit{dig}} $\qfrac{1}{2}$ (\textgreek{ϛ}″).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0680-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L027}{}{\Indent \textbf{\textsf{Pag. 89}}, \textit{inclinationes partium eclipsatarum} respectu eclipticae, quatuor ultimis colum-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L028}{}{nis indicatae. Earum mentio cap. XLIII et XLIV (t. I, p. 101 et 112); tabulae etiam apud}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L029}{}{\Name{Ptolemaeum} VI, 11 (ed. Halma t. I, p. 449), qui secundas etiam praebet, ac \Name{Theonem} I,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L030}{30}{146 et II, 95 (\textgreek{ἡλίου καὶ σελήνης προσνεύσεις}), quorum ope facile fuit parvas codicis mendas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L031}{}{sanare. — \Name{Delambre}, \textit{Astr. ancienne,} t. II, p. 238: « Ptolémée passe ensuite (2) à une re-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L032}{}{« cherche moins importante et négligée depuis par les modernes; c’est celle de la direction}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L033}{}{« de la partie éclipsée soit par rapport à l’écliptique, soit par rapport aux différens points de}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L034}{}{« l’horizon (3). Cette direction change à chaque instant, il se borne à la déterminer pour les}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L035}{35}{« points les plus importans, comme le commencement, la fin, le milieu, l’immersion et l’é-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L036}{}{« mersion. Il fait passer un grand cercle par les deux centres et prolonge cet arc jusqu’à}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L037}{}{« l’écliptique et jusqu’à l’horizon. Le problème est donc purement trigonométrique. Il y né-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-body-L038}{}{« glige l’inclinaison de l’orbite ». Luculentam expositionem harum directionum, iuxta \Name{Theo-}}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0680-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) \Name{Steinschneider}, \textit{Zur Gesch. der Ueber-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-notes_left-L002}{}{\textit{setzungen aus dem Indischen,} in Zeit. d. deutsch.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-notes_left-L003}{}{morgenl. Ges., XXIV, 1870, p. 384, perperam \textit{attacium}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-notes_left-L004}{}{ex \textit{at-tasyīr} (quod directionem seu profectionem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-notes_left-L005}{}{astrologicam significat) corruptum putat!}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0680-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-notes_right-L001}{}{\Indent (2) \textit{Almag.} VI, 11 et 12 (Halma, I, 442-453).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0680-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-notes_right-L002}{}{\Indent (3) Directiones in horizonte vel azimuth indi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0680-notes_right-L003}{}{cantur figura Albatenii t. II, p. 92.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0681}{232}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L001}{}{\Name{nis} Commentarium, praebet alio loco \Name{Delambre} II, p. 597-605. — Numeri manifeste dua-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L002}{}{bus formulis supputantur (cfr. figuram t. I, p. 102 et 112):}
\par\vspace{1mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0681-H03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L003}{}{\CenterLine{sin inclin. = $R\,\dfrac{\text{latitudo Lunae}}{\text{radius \SunSym{}} + \text{radius \MoonSym{}}}$\,, in eclipsibus Solis;}}
\par\vspace{1.5mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0681-H04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L004}{}{\CenterLine{sin inclin. = $R\,\dfrac{\text{latitudo Lunae}}{\text{radius umbrae} \pm \text{radius \MoonSym{}}}$\,, in eclipsibus Lunae.}}
\par\vspace{1mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L005}{5}{Tabulae argumentum sunt digiti eclipsati.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0681-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L006}{}{\Indent \textbf{\textsf{Pag. 90}}, \textit{eclipses Lunae.} Memorantur tabulae cap. XLIII, t. I, p. 101. Similes praebet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L007}{}{\Name{Ptolemaeus} VI, 7 (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 428–29); sed tanquam argumentum tabulae sumit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L008}{}{distantiam veram Lunae a limite boreali, cum intervallo 30′ in maxima longinquitate a Terra,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L009}{}{34′ in minima; numeri omnes discrepant. Apud \Name{Theonem} II, 90, argumentum est latitudo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L010}{10}{vera Lunae, ut in tabulis \Name{Albatenii}; cum quibus etiam commune habet nomen « \textit{morae} »}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L011}{}{(\textgreek{μονή}) pro « \textit{dimidiae morae} » (\textgreek{ἡμίσου μονῆς}) in tertiis columnis (cfr. t. I, p. 275). Sed Theon}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L012}{}{secundas omittit et rudiore calculo quam Albatenii utitur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0681-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L013}{}{\Indent \textbf{\textsf{Pag. 91}}, \textit{eclipses Solis.} Tabularum argumentum est latitudo apparens Lunae (seu latitudo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L014}{}{vera ± parallaxis), sicut in tabula simili \Name{Theonis} II, 90. A \Name{Ptolemaeo} VI, 7 (ed.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L015}{15}{Halma, I, p. 426–27) distantia apparens a limite boreali sumitur, cum intervallo 30′ minuto-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L016}{}{rum; numeri reliqui ab Albatenianis discrepant.}
\par\vspace{6mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0681-H17}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L017}{}{\CenterLine{\textit{Ad pag. 92.} — \textbf{Azimuth ascensionum et occasuum signorum in climatibus.}}}
\par\vspace{3mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0681-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L018}{}{\Indent Haec figura memoratur XLIII capite, t. I, p. 101–102.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0681-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L019}{}{\Indent Similis \textgreek{ὁριζόντων καταγραφή} exstat apud \Name{Ptolemaeum} VI, 11 (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 450–}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L020}{20}{51), sed aliis numeris ob diversam obliquitatem eclipticae. Indicantur amplitudines ortivae et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L021}{}{occiduae initii singulorum signorum, quae formula\quad sin amplit. = $R\,\dfrac{\sin \delta}{\cos \varphi}$\quad deducuntur (t. I,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L022}{}{p. 21). Figura inservit ad inclinationem partis eclipsatae supputandam respectu horizontis;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L023}{}{cfr. antea p. 231. Numeri a sinistra parte radii « \textit{azimuth meridiei} » indicant climata, numeri}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L024}{}{a dextera latitudines climatum; fortasse \Name{al-Battānī} latitudines omnes cum minutis scripse-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L025}{25}{rat, qua re dextera pars ita restituenda: [16° 39′], 24° 5′, 30° 40′, [36° 22′], 41° 15′, 45° 22′,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L026}{}{[48° 53′].}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0681-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L027}{}{\Indent Circuli in 16 partes aequales dividuntur, respondentes praecipuis ventis nostrae, quam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L028}{}{vocamus, \textit{rosae.} Duodecim radiis, intra circulum minimum, \Name{Ptolemaeus} (aut interpolator}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L029}{}{quidam) adscribit ventorum nomina ad illas directiones spirantium; reliquos quatuor radios}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L030}{30}{vocat « ortum hiemalem » \textgreek{χειμερινὴ ἀνατολή} (SSE), « ortum aestivum » \textgreek{ϑερινὴ ἀνατολή} (NNE),}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L031}{}{« occasum aestivum » \textgreek{ϑερινὴ δύσις} (NNW), « occasum hiemalem » \textgreek{χειμερινὴ δύσις} (SSW). Hae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L032}{}{quatuor nomina in \Name{Albatenii} figura (1) ad quatuor angulos paginae collocata sunt, adiectis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L033}{}{Graecis nominibus adeo corruptis ut perdifficile sit eorum restitutio; eandemque collocationem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L034}{}{invenio in \textit{Almagesto Arabico} Bibliothecae Nationalis Parisiensis nr. 2482, f. 128, v., in quo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L035}{35}{nomina sunt: « ortus hiemalis » \textit{al-mashriq ash-shatawī,} « occasus hiemalis » \textit{al-maghrib}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L036}{}{\textit{ash-shatawī,} « ortus aestivus » \textit{al-mashriq aṣ ṣayfī,} « occasus aestivus » \textit{al-maghrib aṣ-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-body-L037}{}{\textit{ṣayfī} (2). Almagestum Arabicum eiusdem Bibliothecae nr. 2483, f. 191, v. circulos praebet}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0681-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) Arabice pluralia sunt: \textit{Ortus hiemales, oc-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-notes_left-L002}{}{\textit{casus hiemales} etc.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0681-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-notes_left-L003}{}{\Indent (2) Ad latera dexterum et sinistrum paginae:}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-notes_right-L001}{}{« occasus aequatoris » \textit{maghrib al-istiwā’,} « ortus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-notes_right-L002}{}{aequatoris » \textit{mashriq al-istiwā’,} sicut in figura Al-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0681-notes_right-L003}{}{batenii supplevi.}
\end{minipage}
\end{document}
